### [1] [DirCast: Live Esports Directing and Commentary with a Single Streaming Video-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31734)
*Zixian Gao,Lixin Xiu,Penghao Luo,Xiangcheng Cao,Ziqi Cai,Zhen Li,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种能够结合导演和解说员功能的单一流媒视频语言模型DirCast，在没有先验剪辑的情况下直接对职业Dota 2比赛进行直播指导和解说，明显优于基于规则的和零样本导演模型，并且其解说质量在Proact-VL协议下也超过了11个顶级流媒体解说员之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的流媒体语言模型只能对已经由人类导演剪辑的赛事进行解说，而不能实时根据未剪辑的原画面自行选择剪辑策略和解说内容。因此，我们研究了如何设计一个既能实现实时剪辑决策又能进行实时解说的模型。

Method: 研究团队构建了一个含有27场职业Dota 2比赛的大型数据集，每个英雄都有一个相机记录视角，并包括了人类导演的选择和解说员的评论。随后他们提出了名为DirCast的流媒视频语言模型，该模型能够以低分辨率扫描所有摄像机，决定切换或保持当前视角，并用高分辨率读取所选摄像机的画面。此外，模型还采用了上下文机制来保持已播出的内容并使其过期，从而控制潜在成本。

Result: 实验表明，与基于规则的和零样本导演模型相比，DirCast在直接直播指导上表现更佳。DirCast的解说质量在Proact-VL协议下赢得了29.2%的比较，而之前最强大的11名流媒体解说员之一仅赢得了19.4%。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了单一模型同时实现实时剪辑决策和解说的能力，表明这种方法能够有效改进直播赛事的解说品质。

Abstract: A live esports broadcast needs a director who chooses which camera goes on air and a caster who talks over that choice, every second and without knowing what comes next. Streaming video language models have learned to commentate, but only over a broadcast that a human director has already produced. We study live broadcast production, in which a model receives the raw camera of every player and must both direct and commentate from the past alone. We present DirCast, a single streaming video language model that does both. Every second it scans all cameras at low resolution, decides with a hold-or-switch director whether and where to cut, and reads the chosen camera at high resolution before it speaks. Its context keeps what went on air and what was said and lets the other views expire, which bounds the cost of a stream of any length. We build a dataset of 27 professional Dota 2 matches with a camera for every hero, the human director's choices and the casters' commentary. DirCast directs better than rule-based and zero-shot directors including GPT-5, and its commentary wins 29.2 percent of comparisons against GPT-4o under the Proact-VL protocol, against 19.4 percent for the strongest of eleven streaming commentators that watch the human broadcast.

</details>


### [2] [AudioLift: Audio-Driven Single Image to Full-Head Gaussian Avatar via Multi-view Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31747)
*Zhuoyuan Jiang,Weijie Lyu,Xiaoyuan Wang,Tiancheng Shen,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AudioLift 是一种无需参数化面部模型或逐实例优化的音频驱动化身生成框架，通过生成多视角视频作为中间表示来捕捉更极端的面部表情。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D 说话头像方法依赖于3DMM或FLAME等参数化面部模型，这些模型的紧凑运动表示限制了其生成极端表情和建模头饰的能力。

Method: AudioLift 采用两阶段方法，第一阶段生成同步多视角视频，第二阶段使用鲁棒的高斯重建来从单张肖像图像中恢复一致的全头几何。

Result: 通过AudioLift生成的3D 说话头像能够捕捉比现有方法更极端的表情，具有更好的几何完整性和全视角多视角一致性。

Conclusion: AudioLift 提供了一种先进的解决方案，将音频驱动的头像生成扩展到无需使用参数化面部模型的场景，显著提升了表达能力。

Abstract: Existing 3D talking avatar methods mainly rely on parametric face models including 3DMM or FLAME, whose compact motion representations limit their ability to synthesize extreme expressions and to model accessories such as headwear. We introduce AudioLift, a two-stage framework that uses generated multi-view videos as the intermediate representation for audio-driven avatar generation without parametric face models or per-instance optimization. Our framework combines a synchronized multi-view video generation stage with an inconsistency-robust Gaussian reconstruction stage to recover coherent full-head geometry from a single portrait image. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AudioLift produces 3D talking avatars capable of capturing more extreme facial expressions than existing methods, with improved geometric completeness and strong multi-view consistency across the full $360^\circ$ viewing range.

</details>


### [3] [SyncVid: World-Equivariant Diffusion for Synchronized Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31765)
*Farhad G. Zanjani,Animesh Karnewar,Fatih Porikli,Amirhossein Habibian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 同步视频（SyncVid）通过在3D空间中同步采样高斯噪声并将其像素化为多个视图来生成动态场景的多个视频，从而实现观测和未观察到内容的一致合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理动态场景的精细几何结构时存在问题，尤其是当这些几何结构需要在多个视角和时间上保持一致时。SyncVid通过在3D空间中同步采样噪声来解决这一问题。

Method: SyncVid 采用在源自视频的规范3D空间中同步采样高斯噪声，然后将噪声像素化到目标摄像头的方法。通过这种方式，算法为视频扩散模型提供了一种结构化噪声先验，该模型不需要进行任何架构上的改变，在渲染过程中引入局部相关性时，进一步提出了跨视图一致的漂白程序以保持一致性。

Result: SyncVid生成了同步视频，并在实验中提高了感知质量和跨视角一致性。

Conclusion: SyncVid提供了一种新颖的方法来合成动态场景，无需改变现有模型的架构，有效地实现了视频中的内容一致性。

Abstract: Generating multiple videos of a dynamic scene from a single reference video requires synthesizing both observed and unobserved content consistently across viewpoints. Existing camera-control methods for video diffusion typically rely on architectural mechanisms, including camera embeddings, cross-view attention, and geometric conditioning. Although effective for static scene content, these approaches often struggle with the fine-grained, dynamic geometry that must remain coherent across views and over time. We synchronize the diffusion process itself by sampling the Gaussian noise prior in a canonical 3D space derived from the source video and rasterizing it into target cameras, rather than sampling independently in each 2D image plane. Our method, SyncVid, lifts a monocular video into a canonical 4D representation, associates noise with scene geometry and unseen space, and rasterizes the resulting structured noise into arbitrary views. Because rendering can introduce local correlations, we further propose a cross-view-consistent whitening procedure that decorrelates neighboring pixels while preserving correspondence across views. The structured noise serves as the diffusion prior for a LoRA-adapted pretrained video diffusion model, requiring no architectural modifications. By enforcing spatiotemporal and cross-view coherence at the source of stochasticity, SyncVid generates synchronized videos and improves both perceptual quality and cross-view consistency in our experiments. Project page: https://qualcomm-ai-research.github.io/SyncVid-Webpage.

</details>


### [4] [PnP*: A certifiable solver for Perspective-n-Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31797)
*Bang-Shien Chen,Caitrin E. Eaton,David M. Rosen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PnP* 是首个能够高效计算并提供可验证全局最优解的PNP问题的最大似然估计（MLE）求解器，通过凸松弛和优化策略确保稳定性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的方法要么解决最小问题，要么将目标替换为更简单的问题，但没有可扩展且能够提供稳定性证明的MLE求解器。

Method: 提出 PnP* 方法，通过凸松弛与利用极少数点进行优化，确保在中等噪声条件下松弛问题是精确的；进一步通过Chordal稀疏结构和商环减少，使求解过程高效且可扩展。

Result: PnP* 在数值实验中能够在超过 10 Hz 的频率下返回一个可验证正确的相机姿态。

Conclusion: PnP* 提供了 PNP 问题稳健且可扩展的解决方案，具有理论和实践上的意义。

Abstract: Estimating a camera pose from 3D-2D correspondences - Perspective-n-Point - is one of the most fundamental problems in computer vision. Existing methods either solve a minimal problem or replace the objective with an easier one. Surprisingly, no scalable certifiable solver for the actual Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE) exists. We propose PnP*, the first practical solver that guarantees efficient computation and returns a certifiable globally optimal solution. We prove that the underlying convex relaxation is exact under moderate noise, which to our knowledge is the first stability guarantee for any relaxation for the MLE of PnP. Furthermore, we reduce the relaxation by exploiting chordal sparsity pattern and quotient ring reduction such that the relaxation is tractable and scalable. The stability and scalability results are verified numerically, as PnP* returns a provably correct camera pose at over 10 Hz.

</details>


### [5] [StackGen3D: Stable 3D Block Stack Generation from Multiview Silhouettes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31803)
*Xiaopeng Zhang,Luzhe Sun,Yiqing Xu,Matthew R. Walter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了StackGen3D框架，该框架通过物理感知的图神经网络和条件流匹配模型，从给定积木库存和三个正交轮廓生成稳定的3D积木结构，实验结果显示，该方法能在保有高 silhouettes IoU 的情况下显著提高物理稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在解决从视觉描述生成物理稳定的3D结构的问题，因为这不仅需要解决几何上的不确定性，还需要考虑到物体间的物理相互作用。

Method: StackGen3D框架分为两阶段：第一阶段使用物理感知的图神经网络（PI-GNN）建模积木之间的支持关系，并作为快速稳定性预测器来构建多样化的稳定结构数据集；第二阶段训练条件流匹配模型，以捕捉与目标轮廓一致的积木配置的多模态分布。在生成结构时，使用轻量级几何选择和细化减少积木间的穿透，而无需进行物理模拟。

Result: 实验表明，物理感知的图神经网络（PI-GNN）相比参数匹配的多层感知机，在从简单堆叠生成复杂结构方面有更出色的表现。Best-of-10 选择可以将物理稳定性从 45.3% 提高到 72.4%，同时保持 silhouette IoU 达到 0.970。

Conclusion: 本文成果展示了结合关系物理推理、可扩展的数据收集方法和条件生成建模对于创造性3D组装的潜在价值。

Abstract: Generating physically stable 3D structures from visual specifications requires resolving geometric ambiguity while accounting for physical interactions among objects. We present StackGen3D, a framework for generating stable block assemblies conditioned on a given block inventory and three orthographic silhouettes. Our approach consists of two stages. First, we develop a physics-informed graph neural network (PI-GNN) that models support relations between blocks and serves as a fast stability predictor for curating a diverse dataset of stable structures. Second, we train a conditional flow-matching model on this dataset to capture the multimodal distribution of block configurations consistent with the target silhouettes. At inference time, lightweight geometric selection and refinement reduce inter-block penetration without physics simulation. Preliminary experiments show that the PI-GNN generalizes from simple stacks to complex structures substantially better than a parameter-matched multilayer perceptron. For structure generation, Best-of-10 selection improves physical stability from 45.3% to 72.4% while achieving a silhouette IoU of 0.970. These results highlight the potential of combining relational physical reasoning, scalable data curation, and conditional generative modeling for creative 3D assembly.
  Project website: [https://ripl.github.io/StackGen3D-web/](https://ripl.github.io/StackGen3D-web/)

</details>


### [6] [SAGE: A hybrid vision-language and geometry framework for work-zone pathway prediction under temporary traffic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31835)
*Jiaxi Liu,Keshu Wu,Weizhe Tang,Bofeng Cao,Sikai Chen,Xiaopeng Li,Steven T. Parker,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE（语义至动作知识导向执行）框架通过分离路径构建、路径偏好和控制规范，利用视觉语言模型的轨迹优选，增强了自动驾驶车辆在临时交通控制下的路径预测与执行能力，显著降低了位移误差并提高了安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于现有几何预测方法难以应对临时交通控制的路径选择问题，SAGE框架旨在通过引入视网膜—语言模型（VLM）来优化路径预测。

Method: SAGE使用了一个读取-决定-执行-检查的框架，其中包含了以几何候选路径构建、路径偏好判断和控制合规性为核心的模块。VLM提供了一条优选路径，而另一个模块则基于这种优选构建多个可行的几何路径候选并根据它们与预期路径的吻合程度进行评估。

Result: 在2,306个真实世界的ROADWork验证场景中，SAGE的生成候选路径在87%的情况下距离人类示范路径不超过10个像素；相比目前基于几何排名的方法，SAGE将平均和最终位移误差降低了8.3% 和12.0%，并且减少了37%的硬约束违规。此外，SAGE在位移误差和安全性指标上优于四种其他适应驾驶的VLM。

Conclusion: SAGE框架成功地解决了自动驾驶车辆在工作区路径规划中的难题，展示了潜在的实际应用价值，同时表明了其在交通安全和效率上的优越性。

Abstract: Temporary traffic control redirects vehicles through work zones, requiring automated vehicles to connect an interpretation of the visible devices to a continuous passing path. Geometric predictors can provide several plausible paths, yet selecting the one consistent with the temporary control remains difficult. We propose SAGE (Semantic-to-Action Grounded Execution), a read-decide-execute-check framework that separates path construction, path preference, and control compliance within a shared candidate space. The central idea is to use a vision-language model's (VLM's) trajectory as a preference over intact geometric candidates. A role-decoupled generator constructs the candidates, and rank fusion combines each candidate's geometric score with its agreement with the VLM trajectory. Supervision structured around the Manual on Uniform Traffic Control Devices (MUTCD) trains SAGE-VLM to state the lane status and hazard side before its passage decision and trajectory. The predicted hazard side enters image-space predicates derived from MUTCD provisions, which support a final intervention bounded in score and displacement. On 2,306 real-world ROADWork validation episodes, the candidates include a path within 10 px of the human demonstration in 87% of episodes, yet selection regret accounts for 53% of the geometric ranker's error. SAGE reduces average and final displacement errors by 8.3% and 12.0% relative to the ranker, mostly in the far field, and hard-constraint violations by 37%. The trajectory opinion improves all four tested generator-selector combinations. SAGE is comparable to state-of-the-art VLM modules in accuracy, with a faster-responding VLM, and outperforms four other adapted driving VLMs in displacement error and safety measures. Project page: https://hhjj233.github.io/SAGE-page/.

</details>


### [7] [IFC-SSL-Bench: Benchmarking Self-Supervised Learning for Annotation-Efficient Multispectral Imaging Flow Cytometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31848)
*Angelo Jovin Yamachui Sitcheu,Roxana-Teodora Fetescu,Thomas Lautenschlager,Nils Friederich,Linus Schilpp,Stephan Allgeier,Luca Deininger,Daniel Hübschmann,Ralf Mikut*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为IFC-SSL-Bench的框架，用于评估自监督学习在高通量成像流式细胞术中的应用，其中包括六个自监督方法在四个骨干网络上的表现评价，结果显示MoCo-v2在ConvNeXt骨干网络上的表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在解决高通量成像流式细胞术中注释专家依赖的问题，并提供一种自监督学习方法来提高分类性能。

Method: 研究采用自监督学习方法，使用六个不同的方法在四种不同的骨干网络上进行评估，评估方法包括下游分类性能和注释效率等指标。

Result: 研究结果表明，自监督学习方法可以与全监督方法在使用100%注释数据时达到相同的性能，但仅需5%的注释数据即可实现形态分类。MoCo-v2在ConvNeXt骨干网络上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一个自监督学习评估框架，并在两个不同数据集上进行了验证，通过对比分析提出了自监督工作流的指导原则，并释放了基准框架供未来使用。

Abstract: Imaging Flow Cytometry (IFC) generates large multispectral datasets, yet the need for expert annotations bottlenecks the development of supervised deep learning models. While Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) offers a solution, its application to IFC remains fragmented and lacks systematic benchmarking. To address this, we present IFC-SSL-Bench, a modular framework for evaluating SSL in IFC. We benchmark six SSL approaches across four backbones on two heterogeneous datasets. By assessing downstream classification performance across annotation regimes of the training sets (1%-100%), we quantify annotation efficiency and identify optimal configurations for high-dimensional biological imaging. SSL matches the performance of fully supervised baselines trained on 100% of annotated data while using as little as 5% of annotations for morphological classification, with advantages in low-data regimes below 5%. Across both datasets, the contrastive approach MoCo-v2 paired with ConvNeXt backbones emerged as the top-performing configuration, with ConvNeXtV1-T best for immunological synapse classification and ConvNeXtV2-T for morphological classification. We identify a clear pattern in transfer dynamics: ImageNet initialization benefits morphological classification, consistent with its visual similarity to natural images, whereas random initialization better captures the more domain-specific immunological synapse classification. SSL elevates class-specific F1-scores by over 35 percentage points for structurally complex classes at 1% of annotated data. We synthesize these insights into a unified set of guidelines for deploying self-supervised workflows in high-throughput IFC, and release the benchmark as a resource for future work.

</details>


### [8] [FSVL: Taxonomic Fast-Slow Vision-to-Language Collaborative Framework for Interpretable Confusable Fish Species Identification and Knowledge Generation in the Middle Yangtze River](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31880)
*Meng Liang,Fucheng Wang,Lang Zhao,Jun Chen,Ziying Li,Yuxiang Zhao,Haozhuang Chi,Wenkai Fang,Qiong Zhou,Junlin Yang,Jinming Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合视觉识别与基于LLM的检索增强生成的快速-缓慢双路径框架，以应对长江流域淡水鱼类细粒度识别的挑战，该框架在保持高效性的同时提高了分类准确性和领域知识生成的精确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长江流域作为淡水生物多样性保护的关键区域，由于鱼类种间形态上易于混淆，且现有深度学习方法难以平衡分类准确性和推理效率，同时大语言模型生成的专业生物知识可能缺乏支持，因此提出了融合视觉识别与基于大语言模型检索增强生成的FSVL框架。

Method: 该方法采用轻量级ResNet50作为快速路径，进行初步分类并引导难以区分的鱼类进入缓慢路径；缓慢路径使用YOLOv8n进行关键形态区域检测，并借助形态学和轮廓特征改善近距离鱼类的区分；此外，框架整合了检索增强Qwen3-14B后台，利用软标记和低秩适应（LoRA）实现参数效率高的领域知识生成。

Result: 快速分类器在测试集中的准确率达到99.67%，级联后准确率达到100%；在255个知识生成样本中，科学名称准确率从91.37%提升到100.00%，属准确率从90.20%提升到98.82%，ROUGE-L值从0.335提升到0.655。

Conclusion: 研究证明了FSVL框架能够在保持高效性的前提下提升分类准确性和领域知识生成的精度，为智能生物多样性和渔业管理提供了有力支持。

Abstract: The Yangtze River Basin is a critical region for freshwater biodiversity conservation. The implementation of the Yangtze River Ten-Year Fishing Ban has increased the importance of effective aquatic biodiversity monitoring and species-level identification. However, fine-grained identification of closely related freshwater fish species remains challenging due to subtle morphological differences. Existing deep learning methods often struggle to balance classification accuracy and inference efficiency, while LLMs may generate unsupported information when producing specialized biological knowledge. To address these challenges, this study proposes FSVL, a fast-slow dual-pathway framework integrating visual recognition with LLM-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). A lightweight ResNet50 fast path performs preliminary classification across 51 fish species in the Middle Yangtze River Basin and routes predefined groups of easily confused species to a slow path for refined identification. Guided by taxonomic criteria, the slow path uses YOLOv8n to detect key morphological regions, including the head, dorsal fin, and tail, leveraging morphometric and outline traits to improve discrimination among closely related species. The framework further integrates a retrieval-enhanced Qwen3-14B backend, using soft tokens and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for parameter-efficient domain knowledge generation. On the evaluated test set of 607 images, the fast classifier achieved 99.67% accuracy, whereas the cascade corrected the two errors, yielding 100.00% overall accuracy; further validation on independent specimens and external conditions remains needed. Across 255 knowledge generation samples, scientific name accuracy rises from 91.37% to 100.00%, genus accuracy from 90.20% to 98.82%, and ROUGE-L from 0.335 to 0.655, supporting intelligent biodiversity monitoring and fisheries management.

</details>


### [9] [StateBridge: Phase- and Track-Grounded Reasoning for Synthetic-to-Real Traffic Video Captioning and VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31898)
*Blessing Agyei Kyem,Richard Dzinyela,Joshua Kofi Asamoah,Eugene Denteh,Andrews Danyo,Solomon Owusu-Ansah,Dickson Gyimah Asiedu,Armstrong Aboah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StateBridge是一个交通视频-语言框架，在仅使用合成训练数据的情况下，在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 2中取得了S2分数55.4679，展示了结构化证据转移对于交通安全管理的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于交通安全视频提供了理解行人与车辆互动的丰富证据，但模型需要从模拟场景中学到并泛化到真实世界的交通情况，因此合成到现实的评估是一个挑战。

Method: StateBridge采用相位感知剪辑构建、行人和车辆轨迹总结以及结构化提示来将视觉-语言模型置于稳定的交通证据中，而不是仅仅依赖于外观。具体方法包括：细调公共视觉-语言模型，使用合成字幕和视觉问答示例，通过事实锁定的字幕融合和证据多样性VQA共识提高不同角色、行为阶段和问题类型的连贯性。

Result: 在仅使用合成训练数据的情况下，StateBridge在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 2中取得了S2分数55.4679。

Conclusion: 通过StateBridge的结构化证据转移工作，表明这一方法在交通安全字幕生成和视觉问答任务上具有有效性。

Abstract: Traffic safety videos provide rich evidence for understanding interactions between pedestrians and vehicles, but synthetic-to-real evaluation makes this setting challenging because models must learn from simulated scenes and generalize to real-world traffic. In this work, we present StateBridge, a traffic video-language framework designed to operate under this synthetic-only training protocol. Our approach uses phase-aware clip construction, pedestrian and vehicle track summaries, and structured prompts to ground vision-language models in stable traffic evidence rather than appearance alone. We fine-tune public vision-language models on synthetic captioning and visual question answering examples, then combine their outputs through fact-locked caption fusion and evidence-diverse VQA consensus to improve consistency across actor roles, behavioral phases, and question types. StateBridge achieves an official S2 score of 55.4679 on AI City Challenge 2026 Track 2 using only synthetic training data. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of structured evidence transfer for traffic safety captioning and visual question answering.

</details>


### [10] [Automated Nucleus Segmentation in Histology Images: An Attention U-Net Approach with Comprehensive Preprocessing and Inference Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31902)
*Akshat G,Divyansh Gupta,Tusar Kanti Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在MoNuSeg 2018数据集上实现了轻量级的注意力U-Net模型，通过图像和掩码预处理以及高保真重叠补丁缝合策略，成功解决了高密度和染色不均一性带来的核分割挑战，IoU得分高达0.8745。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理学领域的核分割对定量分析至关重要，但由于组织学图像中的高核密度和染色异质性，这一过程面临巨大挑战。

Method: 本研究使用轻量级注意力U-Net模型，并结合图像和掩码预处理方法（如去染色和多遍掩码平滑），以及高分辨率图像推断时的高保真重叠补丁拼接策略，解决了上述挑战。

Result: 在未见过的全分辨率测试图像上，模型的IoU得分为0.8745，验证了注意力机制在特征定位增强中的有效性以及拼接方法对于生成无缝和准确分割图的重要性。

Conclusion: 注意力U-Net模型有效地应用于临床挑战性的环境中，实现自动、可扩展的核边界勾勒。

Abstract: Objectives: In the area of digital pathology, nucleus segmentation is an essential step for quantitative analysis; however, high nuclear density and staining heterogeneity in histology images pose a great challenge.
  Methods: This study implements a lightweight Attention U-Net model on the MoNuSeg 2018 dataset to overcome these challenges. The proposed pipeline integrates comprehensive image and mask preprocessing, including stain deconvolution and multi-pass mask smoothing, with a high-fidelity overlapping patch-stitching strategy during inference on high-resolution images.
  Results: The model achieved an Intersection over Union (IoU) score of 0.8745 on full-resolution, unseen test images. This result demonstrates the effectiveness of attention mechanisms in enhancing feature localization and validates the stitching methodology for generating seamless and accurate segmentation maps.
  Conclusion: Attention U-Net is an effective and competitive method for automated, scalable nucleus delineation in challenging clinical contexts.

</details>


### [11] [PixGR: Pixel-Space Generative Decoding for Fast Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31908)
*Ranran Huang,Wenjing Bian,Weixun Luo,Ye Mao,Krystian Mikolajczyk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixGR提供了一种高效的框架，结合了轻量级像素空间生成建模与3D高斯重构，能够直接从噪声目标视图生成场景的3D高斯表示，同时保持前向效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的两阶段管道依赖于VAE进行潜在空间压缩，导致错误传播；PixGR通过直接的像素空间生成监督避免了这一问题。

Method: PixGR利用像素空间流匹配建模场景不确定性，并通过不同的可微渲染来进行端到端优化。

Result: 实验表明，PixGR在稀疏视图设置下优于基于回归的方法，特别是在广泛间隔的观测和视图外推方面。

Conclusion: PixGR通过缓存场景表示并使用轻量级DiT解码器进行迭代推理，保持了前向效率，并且在性能上可与更重的预训练生成模型竞争。

Abstract: We present PixGR, an efficient framework that integrates lightweight pixel-space generative modeling into feed-forward 3D Gaussian reconstruction to model scene uncertainty. Context observations are encoded once into a scene latent representation, which conditions generative decoding from noisy target views to produce a shared 3D Gaussian representation. We formulate this process using pixel-space flow matching, enabling end-to-end optimization of 3D reconstruction with direct pixel-space generative supervision through differentiable rendering. This avoids VAE-based latent compression and error propagation inherent in previous two-stage pipelines. To preserve feed-forward efficiency, PixGR caches the scene representation and performs iterative inference using a lightweight DiT decoder. Experiments demonstrate that PixGR outperforms regression-based approaches in challenging sparse-view settings, particularly with widely spaced observations and view extrapolation. Meanwhile, it maintains sub-second inference and remains competitive with substantially heavier pretrained generative models. Our project page is available at https://ranrhuang.github.io/pixgr/.

</details>


### [12] [Synthetic-to-Real Text-Based Person Anomaly Search via Multi-Model Retrieval Fusion and Cross-Encoder Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31930)
*Eugene Denteh,Blessing Agyei Kyem,Andrews Danyo,Joshua Kofi Asamoah,Richard Dzinyela,Solomon Owusu-Ansah,Dickson G. Asiedu,Armstrong Aboah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种多模型检索和重排框架，通过双向对比学习、显式负样本采样和域随机化等方法对Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B进行适应性训练，结合了多个Qwen3-VL检查点和冻结的SigLIP2模型的评分，并使用Qwen3-VL-Reranker-8B进行最终排名，最终在官方AI City Challenge 2026 Track 4测试设置中达到94.7815%的mAP和90.8493%的R@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于文本的人异常检索模型依赖于合成的图文对进行训练，但在实际应用中，评估是在真实监控图像上进行的，这些图像存在多个域差异，近似帧和大量视觉上相似的干扰物。为了提高合成到现实世界的转换性能，本研究提出了一种多模型检索和重排框架。

Method: 本研究设计了一种适应性训练方法，包括双向对比学习、显式负样本采样和域随机化等技术，并结合了多个Qwen3-VL检查点与冻结的SigLIP2模型的评分。通过四维画廊编码将检索分值融合，使用Qwen3-VL-Reranker-8B进行联合评估，并通过交叉编码器和检索分数进行最终排名。

Result: 通过构建使用合成数据的重排匹配验证协议，本研究优化了检查点权重、融合系数、候选深度和检索参数。最终系统在官方AI City Challenge 2026 Track 4的测试设置中取得了94.7815%的mAP和90.8493%的R@1，排名第8。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的方法提升了合成图像到真实图像的转换性能，并在大规模图像检索任务中取得了显著效果。

Abstract: Text-based person anomaly search requires a model to retrieve individuals from a large image gallery using natural-language descriptions of both appearance and behavior. This task is challenging because model training relies on synthetic image-text pairs, whereas evaluation is performed on real surveillance images containing domain differences, near-duplicate frames, and many visually similar distractors. We present a multi-model retrieval and reranking framework designed to improve synthetic-to-real transfer. The framework first adapts Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B to synthetic person descriptions using bidirectional contrastive learning, explicit negative sampling, false-negative masking, and domain randomization. It then combines the normalized scores of six complementary Qwen3-VL checkpoints with those of a frozen SigLIP2 model using four-view gallery encoding. The fused retrieval scores identify the strongest candidates, which are jointly evaluated with the query using Qwen3-VL-Reranker-8B. The cross-encoder and retrieval scores are subsequently blended to produce the final ranking. We also construct a distractor-matched validation protocol entirely from synthetic data to select checkpoint weights, fusion coefficients, candidate depths, and reranking parameters without accessing the official test images. The final system achieves 94.7815% mAP and 90.8493% R@1 under the official AI City Challenge 2026 Track 4 test setting, with a reported eighth-place finish on the public leaderboard.

</details>


### [13] [LOCI: Localized Continuous Control for Multi-Subject Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31950)
*Sai Vidyaranya Nuthalapati,Shlok Kumar Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LOCI（局部连续控制多主题图像编辑）提供了一种新的方法，实现多主题图像的局部、连续强度控制和多主题的同时独立编辑。相比现有方法，LOCI显著降低了跨区域的相互干扰，保持了编辑强度的连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像编辑方法提供了全局或固定的局部编辑，但无法同时支持局部控制、连续强度调整以及多个主体的同时编辑。LOCI通过结合主体级注意力绑定和区域LoRA层，实现了对多主题图像编辑的精确控制。

Method: LOCI方法通过利用主体级注意力绑定实现空间隔离，并使用一种称为区域LoRA的层来实现连续的、局部的强度控制。通过对单一适配器在特定抑制点进行训练，并在推理时通过缩放其区域残差来实现连续编辑强度。

Result: 与基线方法相比，LOCI在多主题场景的编辑中实现了更高的编辑保真度，显著减少了跨区域干扰，同时保持了编辑强度的连续性。

Conclusion: LOCI为多主题图像编辑提供了一种新的、有效的解决方案，通过局部连续控制提高了编辑质量和灵活性。

Abstract: Recent instruction-following image editors such as Flux.1-Kontext enable text-driven editing but lack fine-grained control over where and how much an edit is applied. Existing approaches provide either continuous but global control or spatially localized editing at a fixed strength. To our knowledge, none jointly supports explicit spatial localization, continuous per-region strength, and independent simultaneous control of multiple subjects. We present LOCI (LOcalized Continuous control for multi- subject Image editing). Given a multi-subject image with per-subject regions and instructions, LOCI edits each subject at an independently chosen, continuously adjustable strength, for instance gradually aging one person while adding a beard to another with minimal cross-region leakage. LOCI pairs subject-level attention binding for spatial isolation with a Regional LoRA layer that replaces the LoRA's scalar coefficient with a per-token scaling vector aligned to the spatial layout. A single adapter is trained at one suppression point, and continuous edit strengths follow by rescaling its regional residual at inference. Experiments on multi-subject scenes demonstrate that LOCI achieves strong edit fidelity with significantly reduced cross-region interference compared to baselines, while maintaining continuous control over edit strength

</details>


### [14] [STEP: Surgical Point Tracking from Sparse Endpoints and Pseudo-trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31951)
*Bing Fan,Anwesa Choudhuri,Zhongpai Gao,Meng Zheng,Benjamin Planche,Terrence Chen,Heng Fan,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STEP框架通过学习手术视频和稀疏物理端点，提供了一种两阶段方法，用于在存在组织变形和遮挡的情况下进行手术关键点跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前手术关键点跟踪缺乏持久的对应关系，需要恒定的注释，导致成本高且耗时。本文旨在提高手术轨迹标注的效率，减少标注成本。

Method: 第一步，使用固定且离线的老师生成候选轨迹；第二步，通过物理端点损失锚定终端对应关系，使用循环往返损失约束时间一致性。此外，非梯度前缀暴露于累积漂移，且最终可微窗口限制激活记忆。

Result: 在STIR-to-VL-SurgPT迁移实验中，时间-立体联合选择减少了物理端点误差7.0%。在29个外置诊断VL-SurgPT片段上，STEP将物理端点误差从31.52像素减少到16.80像素。

Conclusion: 本文提出的STEP框架证明了将精心策划的轨迹与物理观察相结合以训练手术点追踪器的有效性。

Abstract: Surgical point tracking requires persistent correspondence through tissue deformation and occlusion, yet dense trajectory annotations are costly to obtain. We present STEP, a two-stage framework that learns from surgical video and sparse physical endpoints. First, a frozen offline teacher generates candidate trajectories, and temporal reversibility and stereo consistency select dense supervision for a causal student. Second, a robust physical endpoint loss anchors terminal correspondence, while a round-trip loss constrains temporal consistency. A gradient-free prefix exposes training to accumulated drift, while a final differentiable window bounds activation memory. The trained tracker processes a single RGB stream with fixed weights. In STIR-to-VL-SurgPT transfer, temporal-stereo union selection reduces endpoint error by 7.0% relative to unfiltered trajectories. On a post-hoc diagnostic of 29 VL-SurgPT clips excluded from the endpoint-training manifest, STEP reduces endpoint error from 31.52 to 16.80 pixels relative to dense-label fine-tuning. These results support combining curated trajectories with physical observations to train surgical point trackers.

</details>


### [15] [Spectra: Multi-Scale Implicit Decomposition for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31979)
*Dhananjaya Jayasundara,Kartik Narayan,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇文章提出了一种名为Spectra的深伪检测框架，通过结合语义视觉表示和基于隐式神经表示（INR）的感知指标，实现了对合成内容更为本质的差异的检测。实验表明，该方法在鲁棒性和跨数据集泛化方面优于仅依赖于RGB外观或语义表示的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深伪检测方法往往难以在未见的伪造技术、不同的压缩级别和数据集变化中实现泛化，本文旨在提出一种新的框架，通过使用隐式神经表示（INR），能够揭示难以直接在图像领域观察到的重构动态，进而更准确地识别欺骗信号。

Method: 该方法首先利用隐式神经表示（INR）为每张图像建立一个特定的内部激活模型，接着从中提取多层级的重构热图，并将其与语义特征进行双向融合。该融合机制使得图像语义和重构伪迹之间能够直接互动，从而识别出依赖于具体生成管道较少的伪造特征。

Result: 实验表明，Spectra框架在鲁棒性和跨数据集泛化方面优于现有的基于RGB外观或语义表示的方法。此外，该框架还显示出对人脸呈现攻击检测的适用性，具有统一的面部攻击检测潜力。

Conclusion: Spectra框架提供了一种新的视角，通过将语义视觉特征与基于隐式神经表示的感知指标结合起来，提供了对合成内容更高的探测能力。该框架在多个伪造检测基准测试中表现出优越的性能，并被证明在面部呈现攻击检测上有潜力。

Abstract: Deepfakes increasingly undermine trust in digital media, yet existing detectors often struggle to generalize across unseen manipulation techniques, compression levels, and dataset shifts. Most approaches rely on RGB appearance, frequency-domain artifacts, or their combination, learning cues present in the final synthesized image. While effective in-distribution, such cues are frequently generator-specific and can diminish as synthesis models evolve. We argue that a complementary perspective can be obtained through implicit neural representations (INRs), which expose how an image is reconstructed rather than merely how it appears. By fitting an INR to an image and analyzing its internal activations, one can reveal reconstruction dynamics that are difficult to observe in the image domain alone and may capture more intrinsic differences between genuine and synthetic content. To this end, we propose Spectra, a deepfake detection framework that combines semantic visual representations with artifact-aware cues derived from INR reconstruction. Spectra extracts multi-level reconstruction heatmaps from image-specific INRs and jointly models them with semantic features through a bidirectional fusion mechanism. This enables direct interaction between image semantics and reconstruction artifacts, allowing the model to identify forgery cues that are less dependent on specific generation pipelines. Extensive experiments on multiple deepfake benchmarks demonstrate improved robustness and cross-dataset generalization, consistently outperforming detectors based solely on RGB appearance or semantic representations. Furthermore, we show that the proposed representation extends beyond deepfake detection to face presentation attack detection, highlighting its potential as a unified framework for facial attack detection.

</details>


### [16] [Interpreting Physics in Video Encoders using Multi-Object Interaction Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31983)
*Ayushi Mehrotra,Sai Vidyaranya Nuthalapati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在两个自监督视频编码器（V-JEPA2和VideoMAE）中探索了关系物理属性，并分析了这些属性在不同环境下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解释性工作主要集中在单个对象的数量上，但关系物理属性，如动量传输、恢复和摩擦，需要在不同体间定义，并未被详细研究。研究希望探明这些属性如何随实体变化，以及它们是否能在不同的物理环境中传递。

Method: 研究使用V-JEPA2和VideoMAE两个自监督视频编码器，在两个实体的设置下进行实验。通过分析模型在这些条件下的表现，研究试图识别视频编码器在处理关系物理属性方面的特性。

Result: 研究发现V-JEPA2和VideoMAE在识别弹性与非弹性碰撞上的表现一致。然而，在动量违反检测方面的表现不同，且在不同环境中的表现也有所差异。V-JEPA2的动能读取器在环境改变时仍然保持稳健，而VideoMAE则没有这种特性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了视频编码器处理关系物理属性的能力，以及不同物理环境下这些能力的稳定性和变化。实验结果表明，尽管这两种模型都能区分不同类型碰撞，但在某些特性识别和转移上有所差异。

Abstract: Video encoder models are increasingly used as world models for physical AI, where a policy plans by rolling out the latent, so what physics the latent holds bounds what the agent can plan over. Moreover, existing interpretability work probes quantities belonging to a single object; the quantities that make physics relational, such as momentum transfer, restitution, and friction, are defined between bodies and have not been examined. We probe relational physics attributes in two frozen self-supervised video encoders (V-JEPA2 and VideoMAE) under two-body settings and test if they transfer across environments. Our early findings are mixed. Both encoders separate elastic from inelastic collisions reliably. V-JEPA2's own predictor is less likely to predict a collision that creates kinetic energy compared to one that loses it. Momentum-violation detection appears in only one of the two encoders, a kinetic-energy readout survives a change of environment for V-JEPA2 but not for VideoMAE, and neither model holds a friction coefficient that survives a change of geometry.

</details>


### [17] [OpenHome: From Perception to Insight in Smart-Home Camera Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31986)
*Xianglong Wang,Neil Rodrigues,Pei Guo,Yiru Fang,Kevin Beussman,Shuyang Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Liangju Li,Shunyao Xu,Zhongwei Cheng,Wei Li,Lin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了OpenHome，一个专注于智能家庭视频理解的视觉基准，包含2,254个问题和1,118个视频片段，评估模型在稀疏视觉信号和有限上下文下的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准主要基于网络视频，而智能家庭视频的特点不同，缺乏 subtitles 和 narration，视角、光照、场景各异。因此，需要一个专门的基准来评估模型在这种情景下的性能。

Method: 构建了包含多种类型问题的OpenHome基准，涵盖感知、推理和洞察，使用2,254个问题评估32个VLM模型。

Result: Gemini 4 Argon在整体准确率为81.2%，最前沿的封闭源模型在各任务上均优于开源模型，尤其是在洞察方面领先37.4%。

Conclusion: 这项研究揭示了现有VLM在智能家庭视频理解方面的局限性，并为改进VLM模型提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) have been increasingly adopted in consumer smart-home camera systems to interpret recorded videos and provide insights. However, video clips captured from smart-home cameras are different from curated web videos dominating existing VLM benchmarks. Smart-home video clips often do not contain subtitles or narration and are captured from different viewing angles, under various lighting conditions, and in diverse scenarios. However, the capabilities of the model in this domain, under sparse visual signals and limited context, are not fully assessed by existing VLM benchmarks. To close this gap, we introduce OpenHome, a visual-only, four-option multiple-choice benchmark for evaluating smart-home video understanding. It contains 2,254 questions on 1,118 real-world video clips and organizes 10 tasks into three capability levels. Perception evaluates visible content, Reasoning evaluates causal, spatial, and temporal relationships, and Insight evaluates subject intent, supporting evidence and appropriate homeowner responses. Based on the dataset, we describe the task design, data pipeline and evaluation protocol, and report results for 32 closed-source and open-weight VLMs. The best model, Gemini 4 Argon, reaches 81.2% overall accuracy. Frontier closed-source models outperform open-weight models on every level. The best closed-source model leads the best open-weight model by 16.3% on Perception, 22.4% on Reasoning and 37.4% on Insight.

</details>


### [18] [MAGIC: Margin-gated Contrastive Debiasing with Evidence Restoration for VLM Test-time Robustness Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32013)
*Shunshun Gu,Enze Zhang,Ming Pei,Hang Zhou,Xiao Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个新的方法MAGIC，用于提高大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）在测试时的鲁棒性，旨在减轻推理过程中语言偏差。MAGIC通过评估决策边际来选择性地激活文本和视觉干扰分支，进行高效的去偏差推理。此外，MAGIC还在去偏差后有选择地恢复最具信息量的干扰视图，以进行区分性决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对现有方法在减轻视觉-语言模型语言偏差方面的不足，提出了一种新的方法 MAGIC，旨在提高模型在测试时的鲁棒性。

Method: 首先，MAGIC 测量关键标记的预测边际，根据其置信度应用门控函数来识别易受语言偏差影响的响应。然后，对于选定样本，MAGIC 将来自多个文本和图像干扰分支的标记概率与原始标记结合，进行去偏差决策。最后，MAGIC 在去偏差后选择性地恢复最有信息量的干扰视图，恢复证据以进行区分性决策。

Result: 通过在基准数据集上的广泛实验，MAGIC 证明了其在与各种基线方法的比较中的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文提出的方法 MAGIC 在减轻视觉-语言模型中的语言偏差和提高测试时的鲁棒性方面取得了显著的进展。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of test-time robustness scaling for large vision-language models (LVLMs), which aims to mitigate language bias during inference. Existing approaches typically adopt test-time scaling techniques such as contrastive decoding to improve inference performance. Despite the progress, the performance of these approaches is far from satisfactory due to intentional evidence removal during perturbations and redundant inference cost. Towards this end, we propose a novel approach named Margin-gated Contrastive Debiasing with Evidence Restoration (MAGIC). The core of our MAGIC is to evaluate the decision margins of LVLMs to selectively activate textual and visual perturbation branches for efficient debiased inference. In particular, our MAGIC first measures the prediction margins of crucial tokens, and applies a gating function to identify low-confidence responses that are susceptible to language biases. For selected samples, we integrate the token logits from multiple textual and image perturbation branches with the original ones for debiased decision. More importantly, we selectively restore the most informative corrupted views with sharp prediction distributions for ambiguous samples after debiasing, enabling evidence recovery for discriminative decision-making. We further utilize a calibration dataset to actively identify and prune ineffective branches during contrastive debiasing for cost reduction. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets validate the effectiveness of the proposed MAGIC in comparison with extensive baselines.

</details>


### [19] [NUViT: Vision Transformers for Fourier Space Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32041)
*Jeffrey Gu,Robert C. Heeter,Ellen D. Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了Non-Uniform ViT，一种改进的ViT架构，专门为Fourier空间数据设计，通过重新设计图像标记化、数据表示和位置编码，以更好地捕捉Fourier数据结构，优于标准架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉变换器(ViT)在许多计算机视觉任务中表现出色，但未自然扩展至Fourier空间数据领域，如医学成像和显微成像。标准ViT将图像划分为正方形.patch，这不适于Fourier空间数据的频率和相位结构。

Method: 该研究提出了一种简单但通用的Non-Uniform ViT，通过重新考虑图像标记化、数据表示和位置编码来专门针对Fourier空间数据结构。

Result: 实验结果显示，Non-Uniform ViT在Fourier域图像分类、2D晶体晶格识别、MRI注释和电镜蛋白质异质性分析等方面，均能更好地捕捉Fourier数据结构。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为，Non-Uniform ViT架构能够更好地处理Fourier空间数据，为相关领域的应用提供了改进方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have demonstrated strong performance in many computer vision tasks but do not naturally extend beyond the natural image domain. One important example is Fourier-space data, which is used to represent a rich and diverse set of modalities, including medical imaging and microscopy. Standard ViT tokenization partitions an image into square patches on a Cartesian grid, which poorly fit the frequency and phase structure of Fourier-space data. We introduce Non-Uniform ViT, a simple, general-purpose vision transformer that redesigns image tokenization, data representation, and positional encoding to respect this structure. Experiments on Fourier-domain image classification, 2D crystal lattice identification, MRI annotation, and cryo-EM protein heterogeneity analysis show that NUViT consistently captures the structure of Fourier data better than standard architectures.

</details>


### [20] [ACVA-Bench: Beyond Prompt Following in Abstract Concept Visualization Ability for Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32058)
*Mengyan Wang,Jiyuan An,Yining Liu,YaoWen Zhang,Liner Yang ang Erhong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究引入了ACVA-Bench，这是首个针对抽象概念视觉化的基准测试，评估文本到图像模型在无具体参照图像情况下生成准确、多样且差异化的抽象概念图像的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已有显著进展，但模型处理抽象概念的能力未被系统性评估，尤其在没有具体视觉参照的情况下。

Method: ACVA-Bench 利用了心理语言学规范设计，评估生成图像在语义充分性、表示多样性及概念独特性方面的能力，同时提出了一种基于概念语义的细粒度视觉-语义评估方法，该方法将概念语义分解为可验证的视觉-语义问题，通过人工评估进一步确认其有效性。

Result: 主流T2I模型依赖少数典型的视觉模式，显示出在表示多样性和概念独特性方面的改进空间。

Conclusion: 这项研究提供了未来研究的可解释和可复制的评估基础，旨在提升模型处理抽象概念的能力。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have achieved remarkable progress in image quality and instruction following, prompting growing research interest in their capacity for deep understanding that goes beyond literal prompt matching. However, without an explicit visual scheme provided, it remains unclear and has not been systematically evaluated whether T2I models can autonomously translate abstract concepts lacking a fixed visual referent, such as "loyalty," into visual representations that are accurate, diverse, and distinguishable from closely related concepts. We define this capability as Abstract Concept Visualization Ability (ACVA) and introduce ACVA-Bench, a category-balanced benchmark grounded in psycholinguistic norms of abstract concepts, which examines models' ACVA along three dimensions semantic sufficiency, representational diversity, and conceptual distinctiveness under a low-intervention setting that withholds concrete objects, scenes, or visual metaphors. Since abstract concepts lack a unique reference image, conventional evaluation metrics that rely on reference-image matching are difficult to apply. To address this, we propose a fine-grained visual-semantic evaluation method constrained by concept semantics, which decomposes concept semantics into independently verifiable visual-semantic questions to assess the semantic representation quality of generated images, with its validity further confirmed by human evaluation. Benchmarking results on mainstream T2I models show that these models rely on a small set of prototypical visual patterns, leaving room for improvement in representational diversity and conceptual distinctiveness for abstract concepts, thereby providing an interpretable and reproducible evaluation foundation for future research.

</details>


### [21] [Concept Acupuncturist: An Efficient and Precise Concept Erasure Method for Diffusion Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32059)
*Yiran Xiahou,Siliang Qin,Kai Chen,Shengzhi Zhang,Renyang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Concept Acupuncturist (ConceptACP) 提出了一个结合可解释性分析与无推断微调的概念机器遗忘框架，通过构建概念与 U-Net 解码器中交叉注意力块之间的映射，以及基于稀疏自编码器的特征级纠正机制，实现了高效的且精细的概念删除，保持了图像质量和提示保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于不可解释性扩散模型的机器遗忘方法在处理概念表示的分布式和纠缠性质时，存在效率低下和编辑不精确的问题，导致大规模微调时优化效果不佳。

Method: ConceptACP 引入了一个新的框架，将概念与 U-Net 解码器中的交叉注意力块进行映射，以精确定位最相关概念的优化块；通过基于稀疏自编码器的特征级纠正机制，生成针对性的纠正信号作为监督，用于精确的概念删除。

Result: 相比最佳基线，ConceptACP 在标准一步生成设置下的遗忘成功率提高了 36.34 个百分点，同时保持图像质量和提示保真度，并在其他 Stable Diffusion 基础架构上具有良好的通用性。

Conclusion: ConceptACP 通过改进的框架和方法，提高了概念机器遗忘的效率和精确性，适用于实现高效且细粒度的概念删除。

Abstract: Diffusion-based image generation models produce high-quality images but may generate harmful or policy-violating content, motivating machine unlearning (MU) to selectively remove target concepts while preserving general capabilities. However, existing MU approaches often struggle with the distributed and entangled nature of concept representations, leading to inefficient optimization and imprecise edits when applied through large-scale fine-tuning over full diffusion trajectories. To bridge this gap, we propose Concept Acupuncturist (ConceptACP), a novel MU framework that integrates interpretability-guided analysis with inference-free fine-tuning for efficient and precise concept removal. By establishing mappings between semantic concepts and cross-attention blocks in the U-Net denoiser and localizing the most concept-relevant block for targeted optimization, ConceptACP avoids full diffusion trajectory optimization, thereby improving fine-tuning efficiency. To mitigate concept entanglement, we propose a feature-level correction mechanism based on Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), where SAE-derived sparse, semantically meaningful feature activations are manipulated to construct targeted correction signals, which are then projected back to the output space of the corresponding block and used as supervision for precise concept removal. Consequently, ConceptACP eliminates the need for external inference-time modules while achieving efficient and fine-grained unlearning. Instantiated on SDXL-Turbo, ConceptACP improves the forgetting success rate by 36.34 percentage points in the standard one-step generation setting on average over the best-performing baseline, while preserving image quality and prompt fidelity, and generalizes effectively across other Stable Diffusion backbones. Our code is available at https://github.com/Eran-Xia/ConceptACP.

</details>


### [22] [Guided Zero-Shot Anomaly Explanation for Anonymized Crowd Video Monitoring: Context, Categorization, and Spatial Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32073)
*Mulugeta Weldezgina Asres,Lei Jiao,Christian Walter Omlin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为XAVAD的方法，通过综合匿名化、异常类型分类、视觉定位和场景上下文描述，提供可解释且保护隐私的视频异常检测（VAD）系统。该方法采用多模态CLIP嵌入、改进的LLM辅助提示和池评分机制、新型提示引导局部聚焦自聚焦机制以及定制提示工程生成上下文和推理描述，展示了在多个公开数据集上的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习在视频异常检测（VAD）方面取得了进展，但这些模型的可解释性仍是一大挑战。XAVAD旨在构建透明和信任，促进视觉系统在安全和监测中的应用，同时强调隐私保护。

Method: 方法包括多模态CLIP嵌入的使用、改进的LLM辅助提示和池评分机制来识别异常类型，新型提示引导局部聚焦自聚焦机制来定位空间异常热图，以及通过定制提示工程生成上下文和推理描述。

Result: 在多个公开的CCTV和视频数据集上评估了XAVAD的准确性和计算成本，结果显示XAVAD在准确性和隐私保护方面优于现有基准方法，并提供了关于XAI在VAD中的隐私保护权衡的见解。

Conclusion: XAVAD通过将可解释性和隐私保护结合起来，旨在加强公众对计算机视觉系统的信心，同时提供了隐私保护方面的强大解决方案。

Abstract: Despite the promising development of deep learning for video anomaly detection (VAD), explainability (XAI) of these models remains a challenge. Explainable and privacy-protecting VAD builds transparency and trust that facilitates adoption of computer vision systems, including safety and security monitoring. However, anomaly explainability in privacy-preserving VAD systems is underexplored. In this study, we present the XAVAD approach, a unified descriptive anomaly XAI for an anonymized VAD, that integrates flexible zero-shot anonymization, anomaly type categorization, visual localization, and scene context description. We provide empirical and qualitative assessments of multifaceted explanations integrated with full-body visual anonymization for privacy protection. We adopt a multimodal CLIP embedding for videos, using improved LLM-assisted prompting and pool-scoring mechanisms for anomaly-type identification. We enhance Grad-ECLIP for spatial anomaly heatmap localization, leveraging computation and accuracy through our novel prompt-guided patching autofocus mechanism. We employ VLMs to generate context and reasoning descriptions of detected anomalies through curated prompt engineering. We have evaluated the accuracy and computational cost of XAVAD on several publicly available CCTV and video datasets. Our experiments demonstrate substantial gains of our XAVAD over state-of-the-art benchmark approaches and provide insights into privacy protection trade-offs in XAI for VAD. We further present ablation studies and assessments across diverse configurations and modeling alternatives to demonstrate the efficacy of our proposed methods. Code: https://github.com/muleina/XAVAD}{https://github.com/muleina/XAVAD .

</details>


### [23] [RADIANT: Object-Centric Feed-Forward Global Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32075)
*Sarah Zhang,Zhiyang Dou,Minghao Guo,Benjamin Tod Jones,Wojciech Matusik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RADIANT 是一种基于辐射度的交互式对象神经变压器架构，能够从点输入快速生成全局光照渲染结果，表现出显著优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于变压器的渲染器基于三角剖分，导致自注意力机制随着网格分辨率的增加呈二次增长。RADIANT 引入了一种对象中心化的表示法，通过固定数量的表面块令牌（32个）来关注物体的几何形状和排列，从而避免了这种规模问题，同时保持了最终的辐射传输物理不变。

Method: RADIANT 通过一个中间的交互式对象场景变压器和一个视图解码器组件，实现了对 3D 辐射交换的建模和目标摄像机姿态下的图像渲染。它使用不变形式因子注意力，直接将几何距离和表面方向嵌入查询-键点积中，作为 SE(3) 不变的内积，该内积由学习到的区域和材料增益加权。此外，它使用一种基于深度测试的点溅射，使光线跨对象场景令牌交互，适用于主视见而无需网格连接性。

Result: 在相同的模型容量下，RADIANT 达到了 25 dB 的色调映射 PSNR，比 RenderFormer 快 5 倍的训练步骤，而在 600k 步骤后达到了 32.2 dB，分别比 RenderFormer 和 RenderFormer-V2 高出 3.3 dB 和 7.6 dB。此外，RADIANT 还展示了一种大规模场景执行能力，对于包含 500 个对象的 512x512 场景，在 0.83 秒内完成前向传播。

Conclusion: RADIANT 通过其对象中心化的设计和新的注意力机制，为基于神经的渲染任务提供了一种高效的、表达能力强的解决方案，能够实现高质量的全局光照渲染效果。

Abstract: Feed-forward neural renderers synthesize global illumination across novel 3D scenes without per-scene optimization or runtime ray tracing. Existing transformer-based renderers tokenize individual triangles, so self-attention scales quadratically with mesh resolution. Yet macroscopic radiative transport is governed by the underlying surface geometry and object arrangements rather than a particular mesh discretization; finer tessellation can thus inflate token counts without changing the underlying transport physics, motivating a discretization-agnostic and object-centric representation. We introduce RADIANT (RADiance-based Inter-Actor Neural Transformer), an object-centric architecture for feed-forward global illumination from point inputs. By representing each object with a compact budget of 32 surface-patch tokens, RADIANT decouples attention sequence length from polygon counts. In a single network, an inter-object scene transformer models 3D radiative exchange and a view decoder renders images at target camera poses. To capture light transport without materializing O(N^2) bias matrices, invariant form-factor attention embeds geometric distances and surface orientations directly into query-key dot products as SE(3)-invariant inner products weighted by learned area and material gains. For primary visibility without mesh connectivity, camera rays cross-attend scene tokens guided by depth-tested point splats in a shared spherical frame. Across a 1.03M-scene benchmark under matched model capacity, RADIANT reaches 25 dB tone-mapped PSNR in 5x fewer training steps than RenderFormer, and achieves 32.2 dB at 600k steps (+3.3 dB over RenderFormer and +7.6 dB over RenderFormer-V2). We further demonstrate the scalability of RADIANT, which completes a 512x512 forward pass of a 500-object scene in 0.83 s, with computational cost scaling with the number of objects rather than polygons.

</details>


### [24] [ImageRSI: Verification-Driven Recursive Self-Improvement for Agentic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32120)
*Shuo Han,Zhilin Wang,Peng Ling,Wenjia Gong,Yang Liu,Guanjun Jiang,Yukun Cao,Xike Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ImageRSI 提出了一种框架，通过五个维度的验证和修复、共享经验库以及离线更新裁判机制来提高图像生成的质量。该框架在 MindBench 和 ImageReq-Bench 上分别取得了 52.6% 和 65.66% 的 Checklist 严格准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的文本到图像生成方法在处理复杂请求时仍存在挑战，修复单个图像的有效性不一定适用于其他请求。ImageRSI 的目标是解决这个问题。

Method: ImageRSI 框架包括五个维度的验证和修复、共享经验库以及离线更新裁判机制。通过 A/B 验证筛选出的经验被存储在经验库中，并使用监督微调和 GRPO 在积累的图像-裁判记录上进行离线更新。

Result: ImageRSI 在 MindBench 和 ImageReq-Bench 上分别达到了 52.6% 和 65.66% 的 Checklist 严格准确性。此外，还引入了一个包含 961 个请求的基准测试，这些请求基于实际图像生成请求中的难题。

Conclusion: ImageRSI 框架展示了在提高图像生成质量方面的有效性和实用性，特别是通过复杂的、实际应用中的文本到图像请求。

Abstract: Complex text-to-image requests remain difficult to satisfy reliably. Verifying and repairing an image can fix an individual output, but a successful repair does not necessarily carry over to other requests. We present ImageRSI, a framework that keeps the image generator frozen and combines three components: five-dimensional verification and repair within each task, an experience library shared across tasks, and offline updating of the judge. Candidate experiences enter the library only after paired A/B validation, and the judge is updated with supervised fine-tuning and GRPO on accumulated image-judgment records and evaluated before reuse. We also introduce ImageReq-Bench, a benchmark of 961 requests built around hard cases in real-world image-generation requests, each paired with an evaluation checklist. ImageRSI achieves a Checklist Strict Accuracy (CSA) of 52.6% on MindBench (with Nano Banana Pro as the generator) and 65.66% on ImageReq-Bench, and we compare system outcomes across judge-training configurations.

</details>


### [25] [Data Quality in RVL-CDIP: A Systematic Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32126)
*Stefan Larson,Kevin Leach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究审计了RVL-CDIP数据集的质量，发现该数据集存在标签错误和训练集与测试集中的重复或几乎重复文档的问题，提出并发布了解决这些问题的数据集版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计RVL-CDIP数据集的质量，发现数据集存在的问题，并提出解决方案以提高文档分类的准确性和数据安全性。

Method: 通过对RVL-CDIP数据集进行审计，识别标签错误和重复文档，并使用不同方法进行分类实验来评估数据集对模型性能的影响。

Result: 发现RVL-CDIP中的10.5%的文档标签错误，且35.2%的测试集在训练集中有重复或几乎重复的文档。约16,000份文档包含敏感个人信息。重新训练后的模型在部分任务上性能提高。

Conclusion: 通过发布修补后的数据集和注释，提高了数据集的质量和性能，为文档分类任务提供了更可靠的数据基础。

Abstract: RVL-CDIP is the de facto standard benchmark for document classification, and reported accuracy on it is now 98.8%. In this paper, we audit RVL-CDIP for data quality issues and find that approximately 10.5% of its 400,000 documents have erroneous labels and that 35.2% of the test set has a duplicate or near-duplicate in the training set. Identification codes on the pages are sufficient to classify at 69% accuracy, and a state-of-the-art model loses 11 points without them. Image-only models trained on RVL-CDIP lose 16 to 28 points on documents from other sources, and the choice of OCR engine changes accuracy by 4.1 to 8.3 points. We find that nearly 16,000 RVL-CDIP documents contain sensitive personal data, and produce de-identified versions of these documents. We re-benchmark 25 models, and with all defects remediated, the 13 that we retrain are 0.9 to 2.9 points more accurate than in the original condition; the most accurate of them, LayoutLMv3, is 96.5% accurate. We release the corrected corpus, the annotations and the out-of-distribution data.

</details>


### [26] [SyncPro: Synchronous Reasoning and Responding for Proactive Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32132)
*Xueyun Tian,Bingbing Xu,Jiaming Liu,Jinpeng Yu,Yang Shi,Yu Zhao,Ruihua Huang,Yuanzhuo Wang,Huawei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SyncPro框架，该框架采用同步双流结构，通过专门的生成路径进行证据追踪和用户面向的响应。这种结构允许相关证据变化触发内部更新，并让用户响应与持续推理并发进行，从而提高响应时间和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对于如何协调持续推理与及时响应之间的关系还存在不足，本文提出SyncPro框架为了解决这一问题。通过结合固定推理间隔和共享生成路径的方法，使得证据保存和用户请求的响应各自独立运作，提高系统效率与响应准确性。

Method: SyncPro通过引入一种同步双流框架，使用专门的生成路径进行证据追踪和用户面向的响应。Inner和Response分支共享因果历史，独立激活。这使得在有足够的证据时，响应可以同时进行，而无需等待推理全部完成。

Result: SyncPro框架在三个主动基准测试中表现出更佳的响应时间和更准确的回答。

Conclusion: SyncPro通过独立处理证据追踪和用户面向的响应，有效地提高了持续推理的及时性和响应的准确性。 

Abstract: Proactive streaming video understanding requires assistants to track unfolding events and respond when a user's requested condition is met. To make these decisions, assistants must accumulate relevant evidence and reassess response conditions as new observations arrive. Existing work offers complementary capabilities for this process: proactive video assistants learn when to respond, while streaming video reasoning methods generate scheduled intermediate text to organize observations and support subsequent answers. Together, these capabilities provide a foundation for proactive reasoning, but how to coordinate ongoing reasoning with timely responses remains underexplored. In a direct adaptation, fixed reasoning intervals can trigger updates when nothing relevant has changed or postpone useful updates until the next scheduled step. Sharing one generation path also couples two distinct requirements: preserving evidence for future decisions and producing only the response requested by the user. Moreover, responses generated after reasoning must wait for it to finish, even when sufficient evidence is already available. To address these challenges, we introduce SyncPro, a synchronous dual-stream framework with specialized generation paths for evidence tracking and user-facing responses. Its Inner and Response branches share causal history and activate independently, allowing relevant evidence changes to trigger internal updates and responses to proceed concurrently with ongoing reasoning. We construct 189K dual-stream examples and train SyncPro through supervised fine-tuning followed by trajectory-level reinforcement learning. Experiments across three proactive benchmarks show improved response timing and more accurate answers to user requests.

</details>


### [27] [Separating Angular Alignment from Radial Ordering in Hyperbolic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32146)
*Mingyang Gao,Yuwei Wu,Shiyu Luo,Wei Wu,Xiaomeng Fan,Qihao Lv,Haoyu Zhang,Rui Gao,Bingzhen Liu,Zhi Gao,Che Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了AURORA框架，一种解耦了语义对齐和层次蕴含建模的双曲视觉语言模型。它通过角度对齐和一个新颖的径向-角度有序锥（RAOC）来实现，以捕获部分顺序关系并保持层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的双曲视觉语言模型在语义对齐和层次结构建模之间存在冲突的几何约束，本文旨在通过新的模型架构解决这一问题，以提高模型性能。

Method: AURORA框架采用角度对齐捕捉语义相似性，同时使用一个新颖的径向-角度有序锥（RAOC）进行蕴含建模，确保了层次结构的表示，并引入了范数正则化项防止径向退化。

Result: 通过在数百万的图像-文本对上进行训练，本文的模型在零样本分类、图像-文本检索、层次推理和组成理解方面均表现出色，优于欧几里得和双曲视觉语言基线。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了解耦语义对齐和层次蕴含建模的有效性，为双曲视觉语言模型的性能提升提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Hyperbolic vision-language models are well suited to modeling the hierarchical structure of multimodal data, because hyperbolic volume grows exponentially with radius, enabling low-distortion representations of tree-like taxonomies from general to specific concepts. However, existing pretraining objectives for hyperbolic vision-language models entangle semantic alignment with entailment modeling, forcing radial coordinates to simultaneously encode semantic matching and hierarchical structure, leading to conflicting geometric constraints. In this paper, we introduce AURORA, a hyperbolic vision-language framework that decouples the objectives through angular alignment and a novel Radial-Angular Order Cone (RAOC) for entailment modeling. Angular alignment captures semantic similarity while preserving the radial structure for representing hierarchies. Unlike conventional entailment cones, RAOC determines angular allowance from relative parent-child radial gaps. It is incorporated into hierarchical entailment contrastive training to capture partial-order relations. We further introduce a norm regularization term that prevents undesirable radial degeneration for stable training. We train models on millions of image-text pairs and evaluate them across zero-shot classification, image-text retrieval, hierarchical reasoning, and compositional understanding. Our model consistently outperforms strong Euclidean and hyperbolic vision-language baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of decoupling semantic alignment from hierarchical entailment modeling.

</details>


### [28] [Forging Emotions: a deep learning experiment on emotions and art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32163)
*Amalia Foka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文探讨了通过生成对抗网络（GANs）生成视觉情感的方法，提出了利用心理学研究中的情感网络来构建描述高级概念的图像数据集，并强调需要理解GAN的结构以创造能够传达或引发特定情感的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前情感计算主要集中在情感识别而非情感生成，而视觉情感生成需要探索新的方法，特别是利用GAN来生成能够表现或触发特定情感的图像。

Method: 通过实验利用视觉情感数据集和GAN的工作流程来探索视觉情感生成的方法。

Result: 研究发现现有的计算机科学研究人员用来构建描述高级概念如情感的图像数据集的方法不够充分，建议利用心理学研究中的情感网络。单独依靠基本心理学发现（如明亮或暗淡的颜色）不足以生成可视化的情感。

Conclusion: 研究建议未来应致力于理解训练后的GAN结构和组合式GAN，以便创造出真正新颖的能传达或引发特定情感的图像作品。

Abstract: Affective computing is an interdisciplinary field that studies computational methods that relate to or influence emotion. These methods have been applied to interactive media artworks, but they have focused on affect detection rather than affect generation. For affect generation, computationally creative methods need to be explored that have recently been driven by the use of Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), a deep learning method. The experiment presented in this paper, Forging Emotions, explores the use of visual emotion datasets and the working processes of GANs for visual affect generation, that is, for generating images that can convey or trigger specified emotions. This experiment concludes that the methodology used so far by computer science researchers to build image datasets for describing high-level concepts such as emotions is insufficient and proposes utilizing emotional networks of associations according to psychology research. Forging Emotions also concludes that to generate affect visually, merely corresponding to basic psychology findings, such as bright or dark colours, does not seem adequate. Therefore, research efforts should aim to understand the structure of trained GANs and compositional GANs in order to produce genuinely novel compositions that can convey or trigger emotions through the subject matter of generated images.

</details>


### [29] [Real-Time Localisation of Dorsal Anatomical Regions on Dairy Cows in Commercial Rotary Parlours from RGB-D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32174)
*Vineet Prasad,Shiron Melvin,Sheikh Azid,Hai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的感知前端，能够在单一前向过帧中从完整RGB-D帧中找到目标牛、十个骨骼标志点和四个命名背侧区域（肩部、背部、臀部和尾头），并且在T4 GPU上每帧运行时间为26.4毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的自动化监测系统要么对整个奶牛进行评分，要么检测稀疏的关键点，但在拥挤的商业圆盘式挤奶平台上无法获得所需的指定区域掩码。为了解决这个问题，作者开发了一种新的感知前端。

Method: 这种方法使用两个编码器，一个处理颜色信息，一个处理深度信息，然后在单个解码器中有三个头，分别处理骨骼标志点和背侧区域的检测。为了提高准确性，采用了密集区域监督。

Result: 在公共圆盘式挤奶场RGB-D图像上构建的数据集（包含538个标注帧，未标注测试集）上，网络达到PCK@10为0.905，区域IoU为0.70至0.91，每帧的运行速度为26.4毫秒。此外，该模型利用了10个标签帧就能在实际奶牛场中达到PCK@10 0.84，并能以有理的温度读数通过深度进一步传输到热成像摄像机。

Conclusion: 该系统能够准确地在挤奶台上检测和定位奶牛的关键点与区域，并应用于实际的商业奶牛场中，提供了一种有效的监测解决方案。

Abstract: Automated monitoring of dairy cows increasingly measures one part of the body, such as the rump for temperature or the back for posture, yet the region itself is usually placed by hand. Existing systems either score the cow as a whole or detect sparse keypoints, often in controlled walkways, and neither yields the named region masks that such measurements need on a crowded commercial rotary platform. We present a perception front end that finds the target cow, ten bony landmarks and four named dorsal regions (withers, back, rump and tail-head) in a single forward pass over a whole RGB-D frame from a commercial rotary parlour. Two encoders, one for colour and one for depth, are fused late and share one decoder with three heads. On an anatomical region dataset that we built on public rotary-parlour RGB-D images (538 annotated frames, cow-disjoint test set), the network reaches PCK@10 0.905 for landmarks and region IoU 0.70 to 0.91, and runs end to end in 26.4 ms per frame on a T4 GPU, against an 11 fps camera. Dense region supervision is what makes the landmarks accurate: removing the region head costs 0.15 PCK@10. Agreement between the two heads flags unreliable frames without labels. On our own rig at the largest dairy farm in Western Australia, the model transfers with ten labelled frames (PCK@10 0.84), and its regions are carried through depth into a radiometric thermal camera, giving one repeatable temperature per region and cow.

</details>


### [30] [Depth-Dependent Safety Geometry in Vision-Language Models: Layer-Wise Localization and Bidirectional Refusal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32182)
*Muhammad Zeeshan Karamat,Syed Talal Hassan,Christiana Chamon Garcia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种轻量级的推理时框架，用于在冻结的视觉语言模型中定位和操作安全关键的表征，而无需重新训练或修改参数。研究发现早期视觉层支持强双向拒绝控制，而深层层在安全方向上保持分离但对控制不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示视觉语言模型在安全行为背后的内部机制，特别是如何在无需重新训练的情况下解决模型安全性问题。

Method: 研究通过分析Qwen3-VL-4B的逐层激活差异，使用因果干预来展示早期视觉层和深层层的控制差异，并展示了InternVL2.5-4B中的类似深度依赖激活几何。

Result: 研究揭示了早期视觉层通过激活引导沿学习到的安全方向支持强双向拒绝控制，而深层层主要对激活幅度缩放作出反应，表现出安全选择性权衡。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了视觉编码器中的白盒表示级拒绝绕过攻击面，并强调了推理时稳定安全敏感表征的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are widely deployed, yet the internal mechanisms behind their safety behavior remain poorly understood. We present a lightweight inference-time framework for localizing and manipulating safety-sensitive representations in frozen VLMs, without retraining or modifying parameters. On Qwen3-VL-4B, layer-wise activation divergence analysis reveals non-uniform safety geometry across depth. Causal interventions show that early vision layers support strong bidirectional refusal control through activation steering along a learned safety direction, with unsafe rejection spanning 6%-96%, whereas later layers respond more to activation magnitude scaling and exhibit a substantial safety-selectivity trade-off. Deeper layers remain well separated along the safety direction yet are largely insensitive to steering, distinguishing representational separability from causal controllability. InternVL2.5-4B shows qualitatively similar depth-dependent activation geometry despite differences in layer location and magnitude. Our results expose a white-box, representation-level refusal-bypass attack surface in VLM vision encoders and motivate inference-time stabilization of safety-sensitive representations.

</details>


### [31] [DS-BiCA-Net: Pair-Preserving Cross-Temporal Modeling for Remote Sensing Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32200)
*Licheng Zhang,Naveed Akhtar,Siew-Kei Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了DS-BiCA-Net，这是一种用于解决遥感图像变化文本描述问题的网络架构。DS-BiCA-Net将内部图像处理和跨时间处理分别分配到独立模块，并在编码器中保持两个图像为独立流，通过窗户状态空间模块和双向交叉注意力模块，分别处理内部和跨时间方向，每个模块的贡献可以独立测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的RSICC方法在处理遥感图像变化文本描述时存在将两个时间方向混在一起、无法独立评估每个方向贡献的问题，而DS-BiCA-Net通过将内部图像处理和跨时间处理分开处理，解决了此问题。

Method: DS-BiCA-Net采用了一种独立的模块化架构，将内部图像处理和跨时间处理分别分配到独立模块。引入了窗户状态空间模块和双向交叉注意力模块，分别处理两个时间方向并共享投影，所有模块采用了相同的预归一化残差架构，但混合器略有不同。最后在聚合头部融合两个流。

Result: 在LEVIR-CC和Dubai-CC上的实验证明，在经过双向交叉注意力后，内部图像处理展现出优势，两方向交互后优于单独处理。DS-BiCA-Net在LEVIR-CC上获得了82.65的S_m^*，在Dubai-CC上达到了66.72（未使用几何增强）的结果，刷新了最新记录。

Conclusion: DS-BiCA-Net通过将内部图像处理和跨时间处理独立处理，提高了遥感图像变化文本描述的效果，为未来研究提供了参考。

Abstract: Remote sensing image change captioning (RSICC) describes in natural language the semantic changes between two images of the same scene captured at different times. Previous methods commonly merge the pair during repeated processing or combine intra-image and cross-image relations in one operation, weakening source identity and leaving the two temporal directions implicit. Without separate modules for intra-image and cross-temporal processing and a separate path for each direction, the contribution of each part cannot be measured on its own, so it remains unclear which part matters most, and the design of later models has no such evidence to build on. To address these issues, we propose DS-BiCA-Net, which assigns the two kinds of processing to separate modules and keeps the two images as separate streams throughout the encoder. A windowed state-space module models context within each image, and a bidirectional cross-attention module compares the two images along two directed paths that share their projections. The two modules use the same pre-normalisation residual architecture and differ only in their mixers, and the two streams are fused only in the aggregation head, so each module and each direction can be removed and its contribution measured on its own. Controlled experiments on LEVIR-CC and Dubai-CC examine operator interaction, temporal direction and module order, showing that intra-image refinement carries no benefit alone and becomes beneficial only when followed by bidirectional cross-attention, an order that outperforms the reverse at matched parameter count and FLOPs. The bidirectional module also outperforms either temporal direction alone. Our method reaches an S_m^* of 82.65 on LEVIR-CC, the highest among the published results compared, and 66.72 on Dubai-CC without geometric augmentation, the second-highest among the published results compared.

</details>


### [32] [Neural Disjunctive Normal Forms for Learning Exact Rank Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32203)
*Diego Marcondes,Wonder A. L. Alves,Ronaldo F. Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过构造神经析取范式表示并引入平滑的阈值函数，文章提出了一种学习秩滤波器的方法，该方法在保持精确算子评估的同时均匀分布梯度，并使用直通机制有效地进行训练。实验证明，这种方法在图像去噪和视网膜血管分割任务上均优于使用精确次梯度训练的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的梯度学习方法受到结构元素局部稀疏梯度的限制，影响了形态学操作的学习效率。为了克服这一挑战，作者引入了一种新的方法来学习秩滤波器，在保留精确算子评估的同时，均匀分布梯度，以提高学习效果。

Method: 通过阈值分解构造神经析取范式表示，并基于此推导出平滑阈值方程的唯一根作为平滑替代，利用隐式求导，得到具有归一化局部权重的闭式梯度表达式，权重的分布由平滑参数控制。利用直通机制，前向传递使用精确的秩滤波器，而反向传递使用上述平滑替代过程。

Result: 在恒定秩的情况下，该方法可以学习任何秩滤波器，包括中值滤波器，并扩展到秩滤波器的组合、下确界和上确界运算。实验结果表明，在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST图像去噪任务和视网膜血管分割任务中，该方法相对于使用精确次梯度训练的方法具有更优的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过优化损失函数的阶梯函数性质和平滑其梯度分布，可以实现对各种形态学运算的学习，并在图像处理任务中提高模型性能。

Abstract: Gradient-based learning of morphological operators is hindered by locally sparse derivatives with respect to the structuring elements. We develop a method for learning rank filters that retains exact operator evaluation while distributing the derivatives across the window. Using threshold decomposition, we construct a neural disjunctive normal form representation and establish a sufficient condition for exactness with integer-valued images and structuring elements. We then derive a smooth surrogate from the unique root of a smooth threshold equation. Implicit differentiation gives closed-form derivatives with normalised local weights, whose distribution is controlled by a smoothing parameter. These derivatives are used in a straight-through procedure: the forward pass evaluates the exact rank filter, while the backward pass uses the surrogate. For fixed ranks, the procedure learns real-valued structuring elements of any rank filter, including the median, and extends to compositions, infima, and suprema of rank filters. Experiments on image denoising using MNIST and Fashion-MNIST and retinal vessel segmentation show that, starting from a generic random initialisation, the proposed approach consistently improves performance over training with exact subgradients in every setting. In image denoising, our best network also outperforms smooth morphological networks in all settings. On vessel segmentation, the learned erosions recover structuring elements that fit the vessels, as theoretically expected, whereas most of those trained with exact subgradients remain close to their initialisation.

</details>


### [33] [Benchmarking Data-Free Continual Model Merging for Whole-Slide Image Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32207)
*Vu Minh Tran,Doanh C. Bui,Maï K. Nguyen,Khang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究建立了一个端到端的数据无关连续模型融合基准，涵盖特定任务的微调、序列融合和两种迁移学习设置下的推理，系统地评估了六种代表性的融合策略在四种TCGA癌症队列和三种任务顺序下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着癌症队列按顺序到达，持续生存分析需要模型能够集成先前知识的同时维持已学信息。为了评估不同融合策略对全切片图像（WSI）基于生存预测的行为，作者建立了一个基准。

Method: 研究通过控制实验协议，使用C指数、IPCW C指数、遗忘度和反向迁移，在四个TCGA癌症队列和三种任务顺序上系统地评估了六种代表性的融合策略。

Result: 结果表明，没有一种融合策略能在所有评估标准中始终占优，而无截尾调整的评估方法可以显著改变方法的相对排名。

Conclusion: 该基准提供了一个统一的基础，用于开发和评估更有效的WSI基于生存分析的持续模型融合方法。

Abstract: Continual survival analysis on whole-slide images (WSIs) requires models to integrate knowledge from sequentially arriving cancer cohorts while preserving previously learned information. Model merging offers a data-free alternative by combining independently fine-tuned task experts without retaining previous training data. However, the relative behavior of different merging strategies for WSI-based survival prediction remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we establish an end-to-end benchmark for data-free continual model merging, covering task-specific fine-tuning, sequential merging, and inference under both TASK-IL and CLASS-IL settings. We systematically evaluate six representative merging strategies across four TCGA cancer cohorts and three task orders using C-index, IPCW C-index, Forgetting, and Backward Transfer under a controlled protocol. Results show that no merging strategy consistently dominates across all evaluation criteria, while censoring-aware evaluation can substantially alter the relative ranking of the compared methods. This benchmark provides a unified foundation for developing and evaluating more effective continual model merging methods for WSI-based survival analysis.

</details>


### [34] [Angular Supervision for Visual Object Grounding in Autoregressive Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32220)
*Parsa Esmaeilkhani,Longin Jan Latecki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于余弦相似性的可视化措施Angular Relevance ($R_L$) 和一个新的辅助损失Angular Object-Aware Loss (AOL)，前者能够生成比注意力图和Logit Lens更清晰、更忠实的注意力图；后者通过直接监督视觉标记嵌入使其与对应物体概念的词汇方向对齐，从而显著提高物体定位，同时不降低一般VQA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归视觉-语言模型（VLMs）难以将生成的文字与正确的图像区域对齐，现有的解释方法如注意力图和Logit Lens由于文本到视觉注意力较弱和sink tokens，产生的空间解释效果不佳。因此，文章旨在提出改进的方法以提高图像区域对齐效果。

Method: 文章首先提出了Angular Relevance ($R_L$)，一种基于余弦相似性的简单可视化度量方法。然后引入Angular Object-Aware Loss (AOL)辅助损失，监督视觉标记嵌入以对齐于对应物体概念的词汇方向，同时与标准的下一个标记预测目标一起进行微调。

Result: Angular Relevance ($R_L$) 没有任何训练即可在无需训练分割头的情况下优于先前的诊断图和基于CLIP的方法，用于引用表达分割任务（RES）。Angular Object-Aware Loss (AOL)的引入使物体定位在热图中更加精准，并证明了学习到的定位也转移到了未见过的训练类别级别的分割。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法显著改进了视觉-语言模型的物体定位能力，提高了引用表达分割（RES）的性能，且不需要训练任何分割头，同时保持了总体VQA的性能。

Abstract: Autoregressive vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to ground generated text in the correct image regions, and existing explainability methods, such as attention maps and Logit Lens, often produce poor spatial explanations due to weak text-to-vision attention and sink tokens. We propose \textbf{Angular Relevance} ($R_L$), a simple visualization measure based on cosine similarity between visual token embeddings and unembedding-matrix rows. Because $R_L$ is invariant to token norm, it produces substantially sharper and more faithful heatmaps than attention maps or Logit Lens on out-of-the-box models. Without any training, $R_L$ yields competitive referring-expression segmentation (RES), outperforming prior diagnostic maps and CLIP-based training-free methods. To preserve localized object information during cross-modal mixing, we introduce \textbf{Angular Object-Aware Loss (AOL)}, an auxiliary loss that directly supervises visual token embeddings to align angularly with the vocabulary directions of their corresponding object concepts. Finetuning with AOL alongside the standard next-token prediction objective significantly sharpens object localization in heatmaps, showing that the VLM's own visual tokens can be used directly for grounding and improving RES, without training any segmentation heads and without degrading general VQA performance. We further demonstrate that the learned grounding also transfers to category-level segmentation of object categories not seen during training.

</details>


### [35] [ACWM-RM: A Reference-Free Reward Model from Complementary Mismatch Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32222)
*Shibo Hong,Siyu Ren,FengJiao Chen,Zhongyuan Peng,Wei Shi,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究引入了Action and Video Mismatch Benchmark (AVM-Eval) 评估工具，并提出了ACWM-RM 奖励模型，能够同时评估视觉质量和动作保真度，适用于生成视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的奖励信号仅评估视觉质量或动作保真度，并未同时评估两方面，导致生成的视频存在缺陷，因此需要一种能够同时评估视觉质量和动作保真度的方法。

Method: 该研究构建了AVM-Eval 和 AVM-Pref 数据集，前者用于评估 IDM 在生成视频中的可靠程度，后者包含动作和视频不匹配的对，可以同时监督动作保真度（VQ）和视觉质量（AF）。研究人员首先预训练了动作条件下的预测器来学习前向动力学，然后使用 AVM-Pref 数据集对带有加权得分头的联合训练模型进行精调，从而实现对生成视频中动作保真度的评估。

Result: AVM-Eval 的评估结果表明，IDM 在生成视频中的可靠性较低。下游 RL 实验表明，ACWM-RM 在两个标准上都优于其他奖励基准。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入 AVM-Eval 和 ACWM-RM，实现了在不需要地面真相视频的情况下，同时评估动作保真度和视觉质量，为未来的研究提供了新的方法和数据集。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has gained attention for improving action-conditioned world models (ACWMs), as it enables reward-driven optimization of visual quality (VQ) and action-following fidelity (AF) beyond flow-matching optimization. Realizing this potential requires reward signals that reliably rank videos by both VQ and AF. However, existing rewards capture only one dimension: metric-based rewards assess VQ but require ground-truth videos and do not directly assess AF, while inverse dynamics model (IDM) rewards assess AF without references but do not guarantee VQ. Moreover, IDMs, typically trained on real videos, may provide unreliable rewards for generated videos containing visual artifacts and physical inconsistencies. In this work, we introduce the Action and Video Mismatch Benchmark (AVM-Eval), comprising 67.2k pairs that evaluate how reliably IDMs assess AF on real and generated videos. We then propose ACWM-RM, a reward model that jointly assesses VQ and AF without ground-truth videos during reward computation. To train it, we first construct the Action and Video Mismatch Preference Dataset (AVM-Pref) with 506.3k pairs providing complementary supervision: action-mismatch pairs supervise AF, while video-mismatch pairs supervise VQ and expose the model to generated videos. We then pretrain an action-conditioned predictor on real videos to learn forward dynamics before jointly training it with a patch-weighted score head on AVM-Pref to transfer these dynamics to AF assessment on generated videos. Evaluation on AVM-Eval reveals the limited reliability of IDMs on generated videos, while downstream RL experiments show that ACWM-RM outperforms reward baselines in both criteria.

</details>


### [36] [Towards Effective and Transferable Physical Camouflage against Multi-View BEV-based 3D Perception in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.32230)
*Linye Lyu,Jiawei Zhou,Daojing He,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 文章提出了一种名为BEVCA的新型多视图BEV基3D感知模型的对抗性迷彩生成框架，该框架结合了BEV特征的对抗性损失和多视图神经渲染模块，能够有效并可移植地针对不同模型和任务发起迷彩攻击。实验验证了BEVCA在数字和物理环境下的优越性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的多视图BEV基3D感知模型虽然在性能上表现出色，但它们对于对抗性伪装攻击的鲁棒性尚未得到充分研究。现有的伪装攻击主要针对单一视图的2D检测器，这限制了它们在多视图3D感知设置中的有效性。因此，需要一种针对多视图BEV感知模型的有效的对抗性伪装生成方法。

Method: BEVCA框架结合了BEV特征的对抗性损失和多视图神经渲染模块。它能够根据3D目标的BEV特征生成对抗性伪装，并在不同3D感知模型中进行有效的迁移。

Result: 在数字和物理设置中的广泛实验表明，BEVCA相较于现有最先进的基线更优越，并且在真实世界条件下的鲁棒性很强。

Conclusion: BEVCA为多视图BEV基3D感知模型的对抗性伪装研究提供了一种有效的解决方案。实验结果证明了该方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Modern autonomous driving systems widely adopt multi-view Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) based 3D perception models due to their superior performance. Despite their success, the robustness of these models against adversarial camouflage attacks remains largely unexplored. Existing camouflage attacks primarily target single-view 2D detectors, limiting their effectiveness in multi-view 3D perception settings. To address this gap, we propose BEVCA, a novel adversarial camouflage generation framework tailored for multi-view BEV-based 3D perception. Our framework integrates a BEV-feature-based adversarial loss with a multi-view neural rendering module, enabling effective and transferable camouflage attacks across different models and tasks. Extensive experiments in both digital and physical settings demonstrate that BEVCA outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and exhibits strong robustness under real-world conditions. Our code is available at https://github.com/WAVE-AT-IDEA/BEVCA.

</details>
